      1. Car Data API (RapidAPI) — provides makes, models, trims, specs
      2. API-Ninjas Cars API — basic specs data
      3. Other automotive APIs on RapidAPI

      Key requirements:
      - Must have Porsche models with specs (engine, performance, dimensions)
      - Ideally includes images (if not, we'll source placeholder images separately)
      - Free tier or very low cost for 3 models

      RECOMMENDATION: Most car APIs on RapidAPI provide specs but NOT high-quality marketing images.
      I recommend a hybrid approach:

      Should I proceed with this hybrid approach, or do you want pure API-driven content including images?

milestone_3_details_forms:

      description: |

        1. Hero Section:

    - id: "TASK-013"
      name: "Test Drive Booking form and page"
      description: |
        Build the Test Drive Booking page (src/app/book/page.tsx) and reusable form component:

        TestDriveForm Component (src/components/forms/TestDriveForm.tsx):
        - Client component ("use client")
        - Fields:
          - First Name (text, required)
          - Last Name (text, required)
          - Email (email, required, validated)
          - Phone (tel, required)
          - Preferred Model (select dropdown, fetched from /api/models)
          - Dealership Location (select dropdown, fetched from /api/dealerships)
          - Preferred Date (date picker, min=tomorrow, max=3 months out)
          - Message (textarea, optional)
          - GDPR Consent (checkbox, required, with privacy policy link text)
          - Honeypot field (hidden, CSS display:none)
        - Client-side validation with error messages per field
        - Submit to POST /api/bookings
        - Loading state during submission
        - Success state: "Thank you" message with confirmation
        - Error state: display error message, allow retry
        - Props: preselectedModelSlug? (for embedding on model detail page)

        Booking Page:
        - Hero section with heading "Book a Test Drive"
        - Subtext about the experience
        - TestDriveForm component
        - Metadata

        Use React Hook Form + Zod for validation (share schemas with backend).

      status: "pending"
      priority: "high"
      inputs:

        context:
          - "Client component with form state management"
          - "React Hook Form + Zod validation"
          - "Honeypot spam protection"
          - "GDPR consent required"
          - "Reusable: standalone page AND embedded in model detail"

        2. Two-Column Layout (stacked on mobile):
        Left Column - Contact Form:

        - Honeypot field

milestone_4_optimization:
  name: "Optimization & Polish"
  estimated_duration: "Week 4"
  tasks:
    - id: "TASK-017"
      name: "SEO metadata and structured data"
      description: |
        Implement comprehensive SEO across all pages:

        Metadata (using Next.js generateMetadata):
        - Homepage: title "Porsche | Premium Performance Cars", description, og:image
        - Models page: title "Models | Porsche", description
        - Model detail: dynamic title "[Model Name] | Porsche", model-specific description, og:image
        - About: title "Our Story | Porsche"
        - Contact: title "Contact | Porsche"
        - Book: title "Book a Test Drive | Porsche"
        - All pages: canonical URL, og:type, og:url, twitter:card

        Structured Data (JSON-LD):
        - Organization schema on all pages (Porsche AG info)
        - Vehicle schema on model detail pages (Schema.org/Vehicle):
          - name, description, image, brand, model, vehicleEngine, fuelType, price
        - BreadcrumbList on all pages
        - LocalBusiness on contact page

        Technical SEO:
        - Generate sitemap.xml (src/app/sitemap.ts) — dynamic, includes all model pages
        - Generate robots.txt (src/app/robots.ts) — allow all, reference sitemap
        - Add canonical URLs to all pages
        - Ensure proper heading hierarchy (h1 → h2 → h3)

        Create reusable JsonLd component for structured data injection.
      agent: "frontend"
      status: "pending"
      priority: "high"
      inputs:
        requirements: "Comprehensive SEO with metadata and structured data"
        context:
          - "Next.js App Router generateMetadata"
          - "Schema.org Vehicle markup"
          - "Dynamic sitemap including model pages"
          - "Porsche brand information"
        dependencies: ["TASK-008", "TASK-009", "TASK-012"]
      outputs:
        artifacts:
          - "src/app/sitemap.ts"
          - "src/app/robots.ts"
          - "src/components/seo/JsonLd.tsx"
          - "src/lib/seo.ts"
      file_locks:
        - "src/app/sitemap.ts"
        - "src/app/robots.ts"
        - "src/components/seo/"
        - "src/lib/seo.ts"
      quality_gates: ["type_check", "lint", "build"]
      human_checkpoint: false

    - id: "TASK-018"
      name: "Performance optimization"
      description: |
        Optimize site performance targeting Lighthouse score above 85:

        Image Optimization:
        - Audit all Image components use Next.js Image with proper width/height/sizes
        - Add blur placeholder data URLs for above-fold images
        - Ensure WebP format via Next.js image optimization
        - Add priority prop to LCP (Largest Contentful Paint) images
        - Lazy load all below-fold images

        Code Optimization:
        - Audit and add dynamic imports for heavy components (gallery lightbox, map embed, admin pages)
        - Ensure proper code splitting per route
        - Add loading.tsx skeletons for route transitions
        - Minimize client-side JavaScript — keep components as server components where possible

        CSS Optimization:
        - Ensure Tailwind purges unused styles
        - No duplicate styles or unnecessary custom CSS

        Font Optimization:
        - Use next/font for font loading (no layout shift)
        - Preload critical fonts
        - Use font-display: swap

        Loading States:
        - Add loading.tsx for /models, /models/[slug], /admin/*
        - Skeleton components matching page layout

        Create performance checklist component for development reference.
      agent: "frontend"
      status: "pending"
      priority: "high"
      inputs:
        requirements: "Performance optimization for Lighthouse 85+"
        context:
          - "Next.js Image optimization"
          - "Dynamic imports for code splitting"
          - "Loading skeletons for route transitions"
          - "Font optimization with next/font"
        dependencies: ["TASK-008", "TASK-009", "TASK-012"]
      outputs:
        artifacts:
          - "src/app/models/loading.tsx"
          - "src/app/models/[slug]/loading.tsx"
          - "src/app/admin/loading.tsx"
          - "src/components/ui/Skeleton.tsx"
      file_locks:
        - "src/app/models/loading.tsx"
        - "src/app/models/[slug]/loading.tsx"
        - "src/app/admin/loading.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/Skeleton.tsx"
      quality_gates: ["type_check", "lint", "build"]
      human_checkpoint: false

    - id: "TASK-019"
      name: "Email notifications with Resend"
      description: |
        Integrate Resend for form submission email notifications:

        Email Service (src/lib/email.ts):
        - Initialize Resend client with API key from env
        - sendBookingNotification(booking): sends email to admin with booking details
        - sendContactNotification(contact): sends email to admin with contact details
        - sendBookingConfirmation(booking): sends confirmation email to customer
        - Error handling: log failures but don't block form submission
        - Email templates using React Email components

        Email Templates (src/lib/email-templates/):
        - BookingNotificationEmail: admin notification with all booking fields
        - ContactNotificationEmail: admin notification with contact details
        - BookingConfirmationEmail: customer-facing confirmation with booking summary

        Integration:
        - Update POST /api/bookings to call sendBookingNotification + sendBookingConfirmation
        - Update POST /api/contact to call sendContactNotification
        - Emails sent asynchronously (don't await — fire and forget with error logging)

        Environment variables:
        - RESEND_API_KEY
        - ADMIN_NOTIFICATION_EMAIL
        - FROM_EMAIL (e.g., noreply@porsche-site.com)
      agent: "backend"
      status: "pending"
      priority: "medium"
      inputs:
        requirements: "Email notifications for form submissions via Resend"
        context:
          - "Resend as email provider (per scope default)"
          - "Admin notification + customer confirmation"
          - "Fire-and-forget: don't block form submission on email failure"
          - "React Email for templates"
        dependencies: ["TASK-004"]
      outputs:
        artifacts:
          - "src/lib/email.ts"
          - "src/lib/email-templates/BookingNotificationEmail.tsx"
          - "src/lib/email-templates/ContactNotificationEmail.tsx"
          - "src/lib/email-templates/BookingConfirmationEmail.tsx"
      file_locks:
        - "src/lib/email.ts"
        - "src/lib/email-templates/"
      quality_gates: ["type_check", "lint"]
      human_checkpoint: false

    - id: "TASK-020"
      name: "Google Analytics 4 integration"
      description: |
        Integrate Google Analytics 4:

        GA4 Setup:
        - Create GA provider component (src/components/analytics/GoogleAnalytics.tsx)
        - Load gtag.js script via next/script with afterInteractive strategy
        - GA Measurement ID from environment variable (NEXT_PUBLIC_GA_MEASUREMENT_ID)
        - Add to root layout

        Event Tracking (src/lib/analytics.ts):
        - trackPageView(url): automatic on route change
        - trackFormSubmission(formType: 'booking' | 'contact'): on successful submission
        - trackModelView(modelSlug, modelName): on model detail page view
        - trackCTAClick(ctaName, destination): on CTA button clicks
        - trackBrochureDownload(modelSlug): on brochure download click

        Integration Points:
        - TestDriveForm: track successful submission
        - ContactForm: track successful submission
        - Model detail page: track model view
        - CTA buttons: track clicks
        - Brochure download: track click

        Privacy:
        - Only load GA if user hasn't opted out
        - Add simple cookie consent banner component
        - Respect DNT (Do Not Track) header

        Cookie Consent Banner (src/components/analytics/CookieConsent.tsx):
        - Bottom bar with "We use cookies" message
        - Accept / Decline buttons
        - Store preference in localStorage
        - Only initialize GA if accepted
      agent: "frontend"
      status: "pending"
      priority: "medium"
      inputs:
        requirements: "GA4 integration with event tracking and cookie consent"
        context:
          - "Google Analytics 4 per human confirmation"
          - "GDPR-compliant with cookie consent"
          - "Track key user interactions"
        dependencies: ["TASK-007"]
      outputs:
        artifacts:
          - "src/components/analytics/GoogleAnalytics.tsx"
          - "src/components/analytics/CookieConsent.tsx"
          - "src/lib/analytics.ts"
      file_locks:
        - "src/components/analytics/"
        - "src/lib/analytics.ts"
      quality_gates: ["type_check", "lint", "build"]
      human_checkpoint: false

    - id: "TASK-021"
      name: "Optimization tests - Milestone 4"
      description: |
        Write tests for Milestone 4 features:

        SEO Tests:
        - Verify metadata generation for each page type
        - Verify JSON-LD structured data output is valid
        - Verify sitemap includes all model pages
        - Verify robots.txt content

        Email Tests:
        - Mock Resend client
        - Verify booking notification email contains all fields
        - Verify contact notification email contains all fields
        - Verify confirmation email sent to customer
        - Verify email failure doesn't break form submission

        Analytics Tests:
        - GA script loads only after cookie consent
        - Track events fire with correct parameters
        - Cookie consent banner shows/hides based on localStorage
        - DNT header respected

        Performance Tests:
        - Loading skeletons render correctly
        - Dynamic imports resolve properly

      status: "pending"
      priority: "medium"
      inputs:
        requirements: "Tests for SEO, email, analytics, and performance features"
        context:

          - "src/__tests__/seo/structured-data.test.ts"
          - "src/__tests__/lib/email.test.ts"

      file_locks:

        - "src/__tests__/lib/email.test.ts"
        - "src/__tests__/components/analytics/"
      quality_gates: ["unit_tests"]

    - id: "TASK-023"
      name: "Accessibility audit and fixes"
      description: |
        Perform accessibility audit and implement fixes:

        Audit Checklist:
        - All images have meaningful alt text
        - All form inputs have associated labels
        - Color contrast meets WCAG AA (4.5:1 for text, 3:1 for large text)
        - Focus indicators visible on all interactive elements
        - Keyboard navigation works for all interactive elements
        - Skip-to-content link at top of page
        - ARIA landmarks: header, nav, main, footer
        - ARIA labels on icon-only buttons
        - Form error messages associated with inputs via aria-describedby
        - Modal/lightbox traps focus and closes on Escape
        - Page language set (lang="en")
        - Heading hierarchy is logical (no skipped levels)

        Fixes to Implement:
        - Add skip-to-content link in layout
        - Add aria-labels to all icon buttons (social links, mobile menu, etc.)
        - Ensure focus ring styles are visible against dark background
        - Add aria-live region for form success/error messages
        - Add role="alert" to error messages
        - Ensure gallery lightbox is keyboard accessible
        - Add aria-current="page" to active nav link

        Create accessibility testing utilities.
      agent: "frontend"
      status: "pending"
      priority: "high"
      inputs:
        requirements: "WCAG AA accessibility compliance"
        context:
          - "Dark theme needs careful contrast checking"
          - "Forms need proper ARIA attributes"
          - "Gallery lightbox needs keyboard support"
          - "Skip-to-content link needed"
        dependencies: ["TASK-012", "TASK-013", "TASK-014", "TASK-015"]
      outputs:
        artifacts:
          - "src/components/layout/SkipToContent.tsx"
          - "ACCESSIBILITY_AUDIT.md"
      file_locks:
        - "src/components/layout/SkipToContent.tsx"
        - "ACCESSIBILITY_AUDIT.md"
      quality_gates: ["type_check", "lint", "build"]
      human_checkpoint: false

    - id: "TASK-024"
      name: "Documentation"
      description: |
        Create comprehensive project documentation:

        README.md:
        - Project overview and tech stack
        - Prerequisites (Node.js, PostgreSQL, etc.)
        - Setup instructions (clone, install, env vars, db setup, seed, run)
        - Available scripts (dev, build, test, lint, etc.)
        - Project structure overview
        - Environment variables reference (all vars with descriptions)
        - Deployment notes

        CONTRIBUTING.md:
        - Code style and conventions
        - Branch naming and PR process
        - Testing requirements

        API_DOCS.md:
        - All API endpoints with request/response examples
        - Authentication requirements
        - Error response format
        - Rate limiting details

        ADMIN_GUIDE.md:
        - How to log in to admin dashboard
        - Managing test drive bookings
        - Managing contact submissions
        - Managing dealership locations
        - Exporting data (if applicable)

        ENV_TEMPLATE (.env.example update):
        - All required and optional environment variables
        - Comments explaining each variable
        - Example values (non-sensitive)
      agent: "backend"
      status: "pending"
      priority: "medium"
      inputs:
        requirements: "Complete project documentation"
        context:
          - "Cover setup, API, admin guide"
          - "Include all environment variables"
          - "Written for handoff to client's team"
        dependencies: ["TASK-019", "TASK-005"]
      outputs:
        artifacts:
          - "README.md"
          - "CONTRIBUTING.md"
          - "API_DOCS.md"
          - "ADMIN_GUIDE.md"
      file_locks:
        - "README.md"
        - "CONTRIBUTING.md"
        - "API_DOCS.md"
        - "ADMIN_GUIDE.md"
      quality_gates: []
      human_checkpoint: false

    - id: "TASK-025"
      name: "Deployment configuration"
      description: |
        NOTE: DevOps agent is not available. This task prepares deployment
        configuration files that the human will use to deploy manually.

        Vercel Configuration:
        - vercel.json with build settings, environment variable references,
          redirects (www → non-www), headers (security headers)
        - next.config.js updates for production (image domains, etc.)

        Database:
        - Document PostgreSQL setup for production (recommend Neon, Supabase, or Railway)
        - Prisma migration command documentation
        - Seed data instructions

        Environment Variables Checklist:
        - DATABASE_URL
        - NEXTAUTH_SECRET
        - NEXTAUTH_URL
        - ADMIN_EMAIL
        - ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH
        - RESEND_API_KEY
        - ADMIN_NOTIFICATION_EMAIL
        - FROM_EMAIL
        - NEXT_PUBLIC_GA_MEASUREMENT_ID
        - NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL

        Security Headers (in vercel.json or next.config.js):
        - X-Frame-Options: DENY
        - X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff
        - Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin
        - Content-Security-Policy (basic)
        - Strict-Transport-Security
      agent: "backend"
      status: "pending"
      priority: "medium"
      inputs:
        requirements: "Production deployment configuration for Vercel"
        context:
          - "Human will deploy manually — no DevOps agent"
          - "Vercel as hosting platform"
          - "PostgreSQL database (Neon/Supabase/Railway)"
          - "Security headers required"
        dependencies: ["TASK-019"]
      outputs:
        artifacts:
          - "vercel.json"
          - "DEPLOYMENT.md"
      file_locks:
        - "vercel.json"
        - "DEPLOYMENT.md"
      quality_gates: []
      human_checkpoint: true

# Deployment requires human action
deployment_note:
  category: "manual_work_required"
  priority: "high"
  message: |
    DEPLOYMENT REQUIRES HUMAN ACTION:
    The DevOps agent is not available. After all tasks complete, the human must:
    1. Set up PostgreSQL database (Neon, Supabase, or Railway recommended)
    2. Configure Vercel project and connect repository
    3. Set all environment variables in Vercel dashboard
    4. Run initial database migration and seed
    5. Deploy via Vercel CLI or Git push
    6. Verify production site and run smoke tests

    TASK-025 will prepare all configuration files and documentation needed.

# Execution plan - what to start immediately
execution_plan:
  phase_1_immediate:
    description: "Start with zero-dependency tasks"
    tasks_to_queue:
      - id: "TASK-001"
        agent: "backend"
        reason: "Project scaffolding — no dependencies, everything else depends on this"
    note: "TASK-001 must complete before any other task can start"

  phase_2_after_scaffolding:
    description: "After TASK-001 completes"
    parallel_tracks:
      - track: "Backend"
        tasks: ["TASK-002"]
        reason: "Database schema needed by API routes"
      - track: "Frontend"
        tasks: ["TASK-007"]
        reason: "Shared components needed by all pages"

  phase_3_after_schema_and_components:
    description: "After TASK-002 and TASK-007 complete"
    parallel_tracks:
      - track: "Backend APIs"
        tasks: ["TASK-003", "TASK-004", "TASK-005"]
        reason: "All API routes can be built in parallel — different files"
      - track: "Frontend Pages (no API dependency)"
        tasks: ["TASK-010"]
        reason: "About page is static, only needs shared components"

  phase_4_pages_with_api:
    description: "After API routes complete"
    parallel_tracks:
      - track: "Frontend Pages"
        tasks: ["TASK-008", "TASK-009", "TASK-013", "TASK-014", "TASK-015"]
        reason: "Pages that need API data can now be built"
      - track: "QA"
        tasks: ["TASK-006"]
        reason: "Backend tests can run once APIs are built"

  phase_5_detail_page:
    description: "After TASK-013 (form component) completes"
    tasks: ["TASK-012"]
    reason: "Model detail page embeds the test drive form"

  phase_6_milestone_3_tests:
    description: "After all M3 pages complete"
    tasks: ["TASK-016"]

  phase_7_optimization:
    description: "After core pages complete"
    parallel_tracks:
      - track: "SEO & Performance"
        tasks: ["TASK-017", "TASK-018"]
      - track: "Backend Integrations"
        tasks: ["TASK-019"]
      - track: "Analytics"
        tasks: ["TASK-020"]

  phase_8_optimization_

tasks:

  - id: "TASK-001"
    name: "Project scaffolding and configuration"
    description: |

      - Next.js 14 with App Router (src/ directory structure)

      - NextAuth.js with Credentials provider
      - ESLint + Prettier configuration
      - Environment variable template (.env.example)
      - Project folder structure: src/app, src/components, src/lib, src/types, prisma/

      - Accent colors (Porsche-inspired red/gold or similar premium accent)
      - Typography scale with premium serif/sans-serif pairing
      - Custom spacing and container widths for full-bleed layouts
      
      .env.example must include:
      - DATABASE_URL
      - NEXTAUTH_SECRET
      - NEXTAUTH_URL
      - ADMIN_EMAIL
      - ADMIN_PASSWORD

      - NEXT_PUBLIC_GA_MEASUREMENT_ID
      - NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY (optional)

    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Foundation & Backend"

  - id: "TASK-002"
    name: "Database schema design and Prisma models"
    description: |
      Design and implement the complete database schema using Prisma ORM.
      
      Tables required:
      
      1. **VehicleModel**
         - id (uuid, primary key)
         - name (string) — e.g., "911 Carrera"
         - slug (string, unique) — URL-friendly identifier
         - tagline (string) — short marketing line
         - description (text) — full marketing description
         - startingPrice (decimal) — base MSRP
         - category (string) — e.g., "Sports Car", "SUV", "Sedan"
         - heroImage (string) — URL to primary image
         - brochureUrl (string, optional) — PDF download link
         - isActive (boolean, default true)
         - sortOrder (int) — display ordering
         - createdAt, updatedAt
      
      2. **VehicleImage**
         - id (uuid)
         - modelId (FK → VehicleModel)
         - url (string)
         - alt (string)
         - category (enum: EXTERIOR, INTERIOR, DETAIL)
         - sortOrder (int)
      
      3. **VehicleSpec**
         - id (uuid)
         - modelId (FK → VehicleModel)
         - category (string) — e.g., "Performance", "Dimensions", "Engine"
         - label (string) — e.g., "0-60 mph"
         - value (string) — e.g., "3.4 seconds"
         - sortOrder (int)
      
      4. **VehicleFeature**
         - id (uuid)
         - modelId (FK → VehicleModel)
         - category (enum: TECHNOLOGY, SAFETY, COMFORT, PERFORMANCE)
         - title (string)
         - description (string)
         - icon (string, optional)
         - sortOrder (int)
      
      5. **TestDriveBooking**
         - id (uuid)
         - firstName, lastName (string)
         - email (string)
         - phone (string)
         - modelId (FK → VehicleModel)
         - dealershipId (FK → Dealership)
         - preferredDate (datetime)
         - gdprConsent (boolean)
         - status (enum: NEW, CONTACTED, SCHEDULED, COMPLETED, CANCELLED)
         - notes (text, optional) — admin notes
         - createdAt, updatedAt
      
      6. **ContactSubmission**
         - id (uuid)
         - firstName, lastName (string)
         - email (string)
         - subject (string)
         - message (text)
         - gdprConsent (boolean)
         - status (enum: NEW, READ, REPLIED, ARCHIVED)
         - createdAt
      
      7. **Dealership**
         - id (uuid)
         - name (string)
         - address (string)
         - city (string)
         - state (string, optional)
         - country (string)
         - postalCode (string)
         - phone (string)
         - email (string)
         - latitude (float)
         - longitude (float)
         - isActive (boolean, default true)
         - sortOrder (int)
         - createdAt, updatedAt
      
      Also create a seed file (prisma/seed.ts) with:
      - 3 Porsche models (911 Carrera, Cayenne, Taycan) with realistic specs, features, and placeholder image URLs
      - 3 dealership locations
      - Sample test drive booking and contact submission for admin testing
      
      Seed data should use realistic Porsche specifications.
    agent: "backend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "critical"
    inputs:
      requirements: "Complete database schema for vehicle showcase and lead generation"
      context:
        - "3 vehicle models at launch"
        - "Dealerships must be editable via admin"
        - "Schema should support both API-sourced and manually-seeded data"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-001"
    outputs:
      artifacts:
        - "prisma/schema.prisma"
        - "prisma/seed.ts"
    file_locks:
      - "prisma/schema.prisma"
      - "prisma/seed.ts"
    quality_gates:
      - "type_check"
      - "prisma_validate"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Foundation & Backend"

  - id: "TASK-003"
    name: "Backend API routes — Vehicle Models"
    description: |
      Implement Next.js API routes (App Router route handlers) for vehicle model data.
      
      Endpoints:
      
      1. **GET /api/models**
         - Returns all active models ordered by sortOrder
         - Include heroImage, name, slug, tagline, startingPrice, category
         - No auth required (public)
      
      2. **GET /api/models/[slug]**
         - Returns full model detail by slug
         - Include all relations: images, specs, features
         - 404 if not found or inactive
         - No auth required (public)
      
      3. **GET /api/models/featured**
         - Returns top 3 models (for homepage)
         - Ordered by sortOrder, limit 3
         - No auth required (public)
      
      Implementation details:
      - Use Prisma client from src/lib/prisma.ts
      - Proper error handling with consistent JSON error responses
      - Type-safe response types in src/types/api.ts
      - Response format: { success: boolean, data: T } or { success: false, error: string }
      - Add appropriate Cache-Control headers for public endpoints
    agent: "backend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "high"
    inputs:
      requirements: "Public API for vehicle model data"
      context:
        - "These are public read-only endpoints"
        - "Used by frontend pages for SSR and client-side fetching"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-002"
    outputs:
      artifacts:
        - "src/app/api/models/route.ts"
        - "src/app/api/models/[slug]/route.ts"
        - "src/app/api/models/featured/route.ts"
        - "src/types/api.ts"
    file_locks:
      - "src/app/api/models/"
      - "src/types/api.ts"
    quality_gates:
      - "type_check"
      - "lint"
      - "unit_tests"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Foundation & Backend"

  - id: "TASK-004"
    name: "Backend API routes — Form Submissions"
    description: |
      Implement API routes for test drive bookings and contact form submissions.
      
      Endpoints:
      
      1. **POST /api/bookings**
         - Accepts test drive booking form data
         - Validates all required fields (firstName, lastName, email, phone, modelId, dealershipId, preferredDate, gdprConsent)
         - gdprConsent must be true (reject if false)
         - Validate email format, phone format
         - Validate modelId exists and is active
         - Validate dealershipId exists and is active
         - Validate preferredDate is in the future (at least 24 hours from now)
         - Honeypot field: if "website" field is filled, silently reject (return 200 but don't save)
         - Rate limiting: max 5 submissions per IP per hour (use in-memory store, not Redis)
         - On success: save to database, send notification email via Resend
         - Response: { success: true, message: "Booking request received" }
      
      2. **POST /api/contact**
         - Accepts contact form data
         - Validates: firstName, lastName, email, subject, message, gdprConsent
         - Same honeypot and rate limiting as bookings
         - On success: save to database, send notification email
         - Response: { success: true, message: "Message received" }
      
      3. **GET /api/dealerships**
         - Returns all active dealerships ordered by sortOrder
         - No auth required (public, used by booking form)
      
      Email notifications (src/lib/email.ts):
      - Use Resend SDK
      - Send to admin email (from env var ADMIN_NOTIFICATION_EMAIL)
      - Template: simple text email with submission details
      - If RESEND_API_KEY not set, log to console instead (dev mode)
      
      Validation utility (src/lib/validation.ts):
      - Zod schemas for both form types
      - Reusable email, phone validators
      - Export schemas for potential frontend reuse
      
      Rate limiter (src/lib/rate-limit.ts):
      - Simple in-memory Map with IP → { count, resetTime }
      - Configurable window and max requests
      - Clean up expired entries periodically
    agent: "backend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "high"
    inputs:
      requirements: "Form submission endpoints with validation, spam protection, and email notifications"
      context:
        - "GDPR consent is mandatory"
        - "Honeypot field for spam protection"
        - "Rate limiting per IP"
        - "Resend for email, fallback to console.log in dev"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-002"
    outputs:
      artifacts:
        - "src/app/api/bookings/route.ts"
        - "src/app/api/contact/route.ts"
        - "src/app/api/dealerships/route.ts"
        - "src/lib/email.ts"
        - "src/lib/validation.ts"
        - "src/lib/rate-limit.ts"
    file_locks:

      - "src/app/api/dealerships/"

      - "src/lib/rate-limit.ts"
    quality_gates:
      - "type_check"
      - "lint"
      - "unit_tests"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Foundation & Backend"

  - id: "TASK-005"
    name: "Admin API routes and NextAuth setup"
    description: |
      Implement admin-protected API routes and NextAuth.js authentication.
      
      Auth setup (src/lib/auth.ts — extend from TASK-001):
      - NextAuth.js with Credentials provider
      - Single admin user: email and password from ADMIN_EMAIL / ADMIN_PASSWORD env vars
      - Password compared with bcrypt (hash the env var password on first comparison)
      - Session strategy: JWT
      - Protected route helper: withAdminAuth() wrapper
      
      Admin API endpoints (all require authentication):
      
      1. **GET /api/admin/bookings**
         - Returns all test drive bookings, newest first
         - Pagination: ?page=1&limit=20
         - Filter by status: ?status=NEW
         - Include related model name and dealership name
      
      2. **PATCH /api/admin/bookings/[id]**
         - Update booking status and/or notes
         - Validate status is valid enum value
      
      3. **GET /api/admin/contacts**
         - Returns all contact submissions, newest first
         - Pagination: ?page=1&limit=20
         - Filter by status: ?status=NEW
      
      4. **PATCH /api/admin/contacts/[id]**
         - Update contact status
      
      5. **GET /api/admin/dealerships**
         - Returns all dealerships (including inactive)
      
      6. **POST /api/admin/dealerships**
         - Create new dealership
         - Validate all required fields
      
      7. **PATCH /api/admin/dealerships/[id]**
         - Update dealership details
      
      8. **DELETE /api/admin/dealerships/[id]**
         - Soft delete (set isActive = false)
      
      9. **GET /api/admin/stats**
         - Dashboard summary: total bookings (new), total contacts (new), total models
      
      Auth middleware pattern:
      ```typescript
      // src/lib/auth-guard.ts
      export async function withAdminAuth(handler: Function) {
        // Check session, return 401 if not authenticated
      }
      ```

  # ── M2: Core Pages (Week 2) ────────────────

  - id: "TASK-007"
    name: "Shared UI components and design system"
    description: |
      Build the shared component library and design system foundation.
      
      Design inspiration: https://www.porsche.com/international/
      - Dark, premium aesthetic
      - Clean sans-serif typography (use Inter or similar premium web font)
      - Generous whitespace
      - Subtle animations on scroll and hover
      - Full-bleed image sections
      
      Components to build (src/components/ui/):
      
      1. **Header/Navigation**
         - Fixed/sticky header with transparent-to-solid on scroll
         - Logo (text-based "PORSCHE" or placeholder SVG)
         - Nav links: Models, About, Contact, Book a Test Drive (CTA button)
         - Mobile hamburger menu with slide-out drawer
         - Active link highlighting
      
      2. **Footer**
         - Brand logo and tagline
         - Navigation links (same as header)
         - Social media icons (Instagram, Facebook, YouTube, LinkedIn)
         - Legal links: Privacy Policy, Terms, Cookie Settings
         - Copyright notice
         - Dark background, subtle styling
      
      3. **Button**
         - Variants: primary (filled), secondary (outlined), ghost
         - Sizes: sm, md, lg
         - Loading state with spinner
         - As link variant (renders <a> or Next Link)
      
      4. **Card**
         - Vehicle card variant: image, name, tagline, price, CTA
         - Hover effect: subtle scale + shadow
         - Responsive sizing
      
      5. **SectionWrapper**
         - Consistent section padding and max-width
         - Variants: full-bleed, contained
         - Optional background color/image
      
      6. **Typography components**
         - Heading (h1-h6 with consistent styling)
         - BodyText
         - Label
      
      7. **FormField**
         - Input, Textarea, Select, Checkbox
         - Label, error message, helper text
         - Consistent styling matching premium aesthetic
         - Accessible: proper aria attributes, focus states
      
      8. **LoadingSpinner**
         - Centered spinner for page/section loading states
      
      Also create:
      - src/components/layout/RootLayout.tsx — wraps Header + main + Footer
      - src/lib/fonts.ts — font loading configuration
      - Global CSS additions if needed beyond Tailwind
    agent: "frontend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "critical"
    inputs:
      requirements: "Shared UI component library with premium dark aesthetic"
      context:
        - "Inspired by porsche.com/international/"
        - "Dark theme, premium feel, generous whitespace"
        - "Must be fully responsive"
        - "Accessible: WCAG compliant"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-001"
    outputs:
      artifacts:
        - "src/components/ui/Header.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/Footer.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/Button.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/Card.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/SectionWrapper.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/Typography.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/FormField.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/LoadingSpinner.tsx"
        - "src/components/layout/RootLayout.tsx"
        - "src/lib/fonts.ts"
    file_locks:

      - "src/components/layout/"
      - "src/lib/fonts.ts"
    quality_gates:
      - "type_check"
      - "lint"
      - "build"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Core Pages"

  - id: "TASK-008"
    name: "Homepage implementation"
    description: |
      Build the homepage (src/app/page.tsx) with the following sections:
      
      1. **Hero Section**
         - Full-viewport height (100vh)
         - Background: high-quality car image (use placeholder URL, e.g., Unsplash Porsche image)
         - Overlay gradient for text readability
         - Brand name and tagline centered
         - Two CTA buttons: "Explore Models" → /models, "Book a Test Drive" → /book-test-drive
         - Subtle scroll indicator animation at bottom
      
      2. **Featured Models Section**
         - Section title: "Our Models" or "The Lineup"
         - Fetch from GET /api/models/featured (server component, SSR)
         - 3 vehicle cards in responsive grid
         - Each card links to /models/[slug]
         - "View All Models" link at bottom → /models
      
      3. **Brand Story Teaser**
         - Split layout: image left, text right (stacked on mobile)
         - Brief brand story excerpt
         - "Discover Our Story" CTA → /about
      
      4. **Performance Highlights**
         - 3-4 key stats in a horizontal row (e.g., "0-60 in 3.4s", "640 HP", "Since 1948")
         - Animated counter on scroll into view (optional, can be static)
      
      5. **Test Drive CTA Banner**
         - Full-width dark section
         - "Experience the Thrill" heading
         - "Book Your Test Drive" button → /book-test-drive
      
      Page must be a Server Component for SSR/SEO.
      Use the shared components from TASK-007.
      Implement proper loading.tsx and error.tsx for the route.
    agent: "frontend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "high"
    inputs:

  - id: "TASK-010"

    description: |
      Build the About page at /about (src/app/about/page.tsx).
      
      Sections:
      
      1. **Hero Banner**
         - Full-width image with overlay

      2. **Brand Story**

      5. **Innovation Section**

  # ── M3: Model Details & Forms (Week 3) ─────

  - id: "TASK-012"
    name: "Model Detail page with gallery and specs"
    description: |
      Build the Model Detail page at /models/[slug] (src/app/models/[slug]/page.tsx).
      
      Features:
      
      1. **Hero Section**
         - Full-width hero image of the model
         - Model name, tagline, starting price
         - "Book a Test Drive" CTA
      
      2. **Image Gallery**
         - Thumbnail strip + main image viewer
         - Click thumbnail to change main image
         - Categories: Exterior, Interior, Detail (filterable tabs)
         - Lazy loading for images
         - Optional: lightbox/fullscreen view on click
         - Use Next.js Image component for optimization
      
      3. **Specifications Table**
         - Grouped by category (Performance, Engine, Dimensions, etc.)
         - Clean table/grid layout
         - Expandable sections or tabs for categories
      
      4. **Features Highlights**

         - Icon + title + description
         - Categories: Technology, Safety, Comfort, Performance

         - Styled as secondary CTA
      
      6. **Embedded Test Drive Form**
         - Inline test drive booking form (reuse form component from TASK-013)
         - Pre-selected model
      
      Data fetching:
      - Server Component
      - Fetch from GET /api/models/[slug]
      - generateStaticParams() for static generation of known slugs
      - generateMetadata() with model-specific title, description, and OG image
      - notFound() if model doesn't exist
      
      Components to create:
      - src/components/models/ImageGallery.tsx (Client Component for interactivity)
      - src/components/models/SpecsTable.tsx
      - src/components/models/FeatureGrid.tsx
      - src/components/models/ModelHero.tsx

  - id: "TASK-013"
    name: "Test Drive Booking form and page"
    description: |
      Build the Test Drive Booking form component and standalone page.
      
      Page: /book-test-drive (src/app/book-test-drive/page.tsx)
      
      Form Component: src/components/forms/TestDriveForm.tsx (Client Component)
      
      Form fields:
      - First Name (text, required)
      - Last Name (text, required)
      - Email (email, required)
      - Phone (tel, required)
      - Preferred Model (select, required) — fetched from /api/models
      - Dealership Location (select, required) — fetched from /api/dealerships
      - Preferred Date (date picker, required, must be future date, min 24h from now)
      - GDPR Consent (checkbox, required) — "I agree to the processing of my personal data..."
      - Honeypot field (hidden "website" field, CSS hidden, not display:none)
      
      Behavior:
      - Client-side validation using Zod (reuse schemas from backend if possible)
      - Real-time field validation on blur
      - Submit to POST /api/bookings
      - Loading state on submit button
      - Success state: show confirmation message, reset form
      - Error state: show error message, keep form data
      - Accessible: proper labels, aria-describedby for errors, focus management
      
      The form component should accept optional props:
      - preselectedModelSlug?: string (for embedding on model detail page)
      - compact?: boolean (for embedded version, fewer visual elements)
      
      Standalone page includes:
      - Hero section with heading "Book a Test Drive"
      - Brief description
      - The form component
      - generateMetadata()
      
      Date picker: use native HTML date input styled with Tailwind (simplest approach per human preference). Set min attribute to tomorrow's date.
    agent: "frontend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "high"
    inputs:
      requirements: "Test drive booking form with validation, spam protection, and accessibility"
      context:
        - "Client Component for form interactivity"
        - "Reusable: standalone page AND embedded in model detail"
        - "Zod validation matching backend schemas"
        - "Native date input (simplest approach)"
        - "Honeypot field for spam"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-007"
        - "TASK-004"
    outputs:
      artifacts:
        - "src/app/book-test-drive/page.tsx"
        - "src/components/forms/TestDriveForm.tsx"
    file_locks:
      - "src/app/book-test-drive/"
      - "src/components/forms/TestDriveForm.tsx"
    quality_gates:
      - "type_check"
      - "lint"
      - "build"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Model Details & Forms"

      1. **Metadata for all pages** (using generateMetadata in each page.tsx):
         - Homepage: brand-level title, description, OG image
         - Models listing: "Explore Our Models | Porsche"
         - Model detail: dynamic per model with model image as OG
         - About: brand story metadata
         - Contact: contact page metadata
         - Book Test Drive: booking page metadata
      
      2. **Structured Data (JSON-LD)**
         - src/components/seo/JsonLd.tsx — reusable component
         - Homepage: Organization schema
         - Model detail pages: Vehicle schema (Schema.org/Car)
           - name, brand, model, vehicleConfiguration
           - offers (price)
           - image
         - Contact page: LocalBusiness schema with address
      
      3. **Sitemap** (src/app/sitemap.ts)
         - Next.js built-in sitemap generation
         - Include all static pages
         - Include all model detail pages (dynamic)
         - Set appropriate changeFrequency and priority
      
      4. **Robots.txt** (src/app/robots.ts)
         - Allow all crawlers
         - Disallow /admin/*
         - Disallow /api/*
         - Reference sitemap URL
      
      5. **Open Graph & Twitter Cards**
         - OG image for each page (can use model hero images)
         - Twitter card meta tags
         - Default OG image for pages without specific images
      
      6. **Canonical URLs**
         - Set canonical URL for each page
         - Prevent duplicate content issues
    agent: "frontend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "medium"
    inputs:
      requirements: "Comprehensive SEO with metadata, structured data, sitemap, and robots.txt"
      context:
        - "Next.js App Router metadata API"
        - "Schema.org Vehicle markup for model pages"
        - "Block admin and API routes from crawlers"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-008"
        - "TASK-009"
        - "TASK-012"
        - "TASK-010"
        - "TASK-014"
    outputs:
      artifacts:

        - "src/app/sitemap.ts"
        - "src/app/robots.ts"
        - "src/lib/metadata.ts"
    file_locks:

      - "build"

  - id: "TASK-018"
    name: "Performance optimization"
    description: |
      Optimize the site for performance targeting Lighthouse score above 85.
      
      Tasks:
      
      1. **Image Optimization**
         - Audit all Image components use next/image correctly
         - Add proper width/height or fill props
         - Use priority prop for above-the-fold images (hero)
         - Ensure WebP/AVIF format serving
         - Add blur placeholder for large images (blurDataURL)
         - Lazy load below-the-fold images
      
      2. **Font Optimization**
         - Use next/font for font loading
         - Preload critical fonts
         - Use font-display: swap
      
      3. **Code Splitting**
         - Verify dynamic imports for heavy components (image gallery, map)
         - Use next/dynamic for client components that aren't needed immediately
         - Analyze bundle with @next/bundle-analyzer
      
      4. **CSS Optimization**
         - Ensure Tailwind purges unused styles (default in production)
         - Minimize custom CSS
         - Critical CSS is inlined by Next.js automatically
      
      5. **Caching Headers**
         - Static assets: immutable, long cache
         - API responses: appropriate Cache-Control
         - Add next.config.js headers configuration
      
      6. **Loading States**
         - Verify all routes have loading.tsx
         - Skeleton screens for content-heavy pages
         - Suspense boundaries for async components
      
      7. **Core Web Vitals**
         - LCP: ensure hero image loads fast (priority, preload)
         - FID/INP: minimize JavaScript on initial load
         - CLS: all images have dimensions, no layout shifts
      
      Create a performance checklist document.
    agent: "frontend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "medium"
    inputs:
      requirements: "Performance optimization for Lighthouse 85+"
      context:
        - "Focus on Core Web Vitals"
        - "Next.js built-in optimizations"
        - "Image-heavy site needs careful optimization"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-008"
        - "TASK-009"
        - "TASK-012"
    outputs:
      artifacts:
        - "src/app/models/[slug]/loading.tsx"
        - "next.config.js"
        - "docs/performance-checklist.md"
    file_locks:
      - "next.config.js"
      - "docs/"
    quality_gates:
      - "build"
      - "lighthouse_check"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Optimization & Polish"

  - id: "TASK-019"
    name: "Google Analytics 4 integration"
    description: |
      Integrate Google Analytics 4 with event tracking.
      
      Implementation:
      
      1. **GA4 Script Loading** (src/components/analytics/GoogleAnalytics.tsx)
         - Load gtag.js script via next/script with strategy="afterInteractive"
         - Use NEXT_PUBLIC_GA_MEASUREMENT_ID env var
         - Only load in production (check NODE_ENV)
         - Include in root layout
      
      2. **Page View Tracking**
         - Automatic page view tracking via gtag
         - Track route changes in App Router (use usePathname + useEffect)
         - src/components/analytics/PageViewTracker.tsx (Client Component)
      
      3. **Event Tracking** (src/lib/analytics.ts)
         - Helper functions for custom events:
           - trackFormSubmission(formType: 'test_drive' | 'contact')
           - trackModelView(modelSlug: string)
           - trackBrochureDownload(modelSlug: string)
           - trackCTAClick(ctaName: string, location: string)
         - Call these from form success handlers and CTA click handlers
      
      4. **Cookie Consent Consideration**
         - Add a simple cookie consent banner (src/components/analytics/CookieConsent.tsx)
         - Store consent in localStorage
         - Only initialize GA4 after consent
         - GDPR compliant
      
      Keep it simple — no complex consent management platform.
    agent: "frontend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "low"
    inputs:
      requirements: "GA4 integration with event tracking and cookie consent"
      context:
        - "Only load in production"
        - "GDPR compliant with cookie consent"
        - "Track key user actions"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-007"
        - "TASK-008"
    outputs:
      artifacts:
        - "src/components/analytics/GoogleAnalytics.tsx"
        - "src/components/analytics/PageViewTracker.tsx"
        - "src/components/analytics/CookieConsent.tsx"
        - "src/lib/analytics.ts"
    file_locks:
      - "src/components/analytics/"
      - "src/lib/analytics.ts"
    quality_gates:
      - "type_check"
      - "lint"
      - "build"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Optimization & Polish"

  - id: "TASK-021"
    name: "End-to-end test suite"
    description: |
      Write end-to-end tests using Playwright.
      
      Test scenarios:
      
      1. **tests/e2e/navigation.spec.ts**
         - Navigate through all public pages via header links
         - Mobile navigation menu works
         - Footer links work
         - Back to site link from admin works
      
      2. **tests/e2e/homepage.spec.ts**
         - Hero section renders with CTAs
         - Featured models load and display
         - CTA buttons navigate correctly
      
      3. **tests/e2e/models.spec.ts**
         - Models grid page loads with all models
         - Click model card navigates to detail page
         - Detail page shows gallery, specs, features
         - Image gallery interaction works
         - Brochure download link works (if present)
      
      4. **tests/e2e/test-drive-booking.spec.ts**
         - Navigate to booking page
         - Fill form with valid data
         - Submit and see success message
         - Try submitting with invalid data, see validation errors
         - Try submitting without GDPR consent
      
      5. **tests/e2e/contact.spec.ts**
         - Navigate to contact page
         - Map embed is visible
         - Fill and submit contact form
         - Validation errors display correctly
      
      6. **tests/e2e/admin.spec.ts**
         - Navigate to /admin, redirected to login
         - Login with valid credentials
         - Dashboard shows stats
         - View bookings list
         - Update booking status
         - View contacts list
         - Manage dealerships (add, edit, deactivate)
         - Logout
      
      7. **tests/e2e/seo.spec.ts**
         - Check meta tags on key pages
         - Verify sitemap.xml is accessible
         - Verify robots.txt is accessible
      
      8. **tests/e2e/responsive.spec.ts**
         - Test key pages at mobile (375px), tablet (768px), desktop (1440px)
         - Verify layout changes appropriately
         - Mobile menu works
      
      Setup:
      - playwright.config.ts
      - Use test database with seed data
      - Configure base URL
    agent: "qa"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "high"
    inputs:
      requirements: "Comprehensive E2E test suite with Playwright"
      context:
        - "Test all user journeys"
        - "Test responsive layouts"
        - "Test admin workflows"
        - "Use seeded test data"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-008"
        - "TASK-009"
        - "TASK-010"
        - "TASK-012"
        - "TASK-013"
        - "TASK-014"
        - "TASK-015"
        - "TASK-017"
    outputs:
      artifacts:
        - "playwright.config.ts"
        - "tests/e2e/navigation.spec.ts"
        - "tests/e2e/homepage.spec.ts"
        - "tests/e2e/models.spec.ts"
        - "tests/e2e/test-drive-booking.spec.ts"
        - "tests/e2e/contact.spec.ts"
        - "tests/e2e/admin.spec.ts"
        - "tests/e2e/seo.spec.ts"
        - "tests/e2e/responsive.spec.ts"
    file_locks:
      - "tests/e2e/"
      - "playwright.config.ts"
    quality_gates:
      - "e2e_tests_pass"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "QA & Deployment Prep"

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# MILESTONE 1: Foundation & Backend (Week 1)

tasks:
  # === TASK 1: Project Scaffolding ===
  - id: "TASK-001"
    name: "Project scaffolding and configuration"
    description: |
      Initialize Next.js 14 App Router project with TypeScript, Tailwind CSS, Prisma ORM, and NextAuth.js.
      
      Setup includes:
      - Next.js 14 with App Router, TypeScript strict mode
      - Tailwind CSS with custom theme (dark premium palette inspired by porsche.com: blacks, dark grays, white text, accent gold/silver)
      - Prisma ORM configured for PostgreSQL
      - NextAuth.js with Credentials provider (single admin user from env vars)
      - ESLint + Prettier configuration
      - Environment variable template (.env.example) with all required vars
      - Project folder structure: src/app, src/components, src/lib, src/types, prisma/
      - Resend SDK installed and configured
      - Google Analytics 4 script setup (via next/script)
      
      Design tokens to establish:
      - Colors: primary-black (#0a0a0a), surface-dark (#1a1a1a), surface-card (#242424), text-primary (#ffffff), text-secondary (#a0a0a0), accent (#c4a35a gold), accent-secondary (#d4d4d4 silver), error (#ef4444), success (#22c55e)
      - Typography: Headings — Outfit (Google Font), Body — Inter (Google Font)
      - Spacing scale: Tailwind defaults extended with custom hero/section spacing
      - Border radius: minimal (2px-4px) for premium feel
      - Transitions: 300ms ease for hovers, 500ms for scroll reveals
    agent: "backend"

      - "build"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Foundation & Backend"

  # === TASK 2: Database Schema ===
  - id: "TASK-002"
    name: "Database schema design and seed data"
    description: |
      Design and implement the complete Prisma schema for the application.
      
      Tables required:
      
      1. **VehicleModel**
         - id (uuid, primary key)
         - name (string) — e.g., "911 Carrera"
         - slug (string, unique) — URL-friendly identifier
         - tagline (string) — short marketing line
         - description (text) — full marketing description
         - startingPrice (decimal) — starting MSRP
         - category (string) — e.g., "Sports Car", "SUV", "Sedan"
         - heroImage (string) — URL to main image
         - brochureUrl (string, optional) — PDF download link
         - isActive (boolean, default true)
         - sortOrder (int) — display ordering
         - createdAt, updatedAt timestamps
      
      2. **VehicleImage**
         - id (uuid)
         - modelId (FK to VehicleModel)
         - url (string)
         - alt (string)
         - category (enum: EXTERIOR, INTERIOR, DETAIL, LIFESTYLE)
         - sortOrder (int)
      
      3. **VehicleSpec**
         - id (uuid)
         - modelId (FK to VehicleModel)
         - category (string) — e.g., "Performance", "Dimensions", "Engine"
         - label (string) — e.g., "Top Speed"
         - value (string) — e.g., "182 mph"
         - sortOrder (int)
      
      4. **VehicleFeature**
         - id (uuid)
         - modelId (FK to VehicleModel)
         - category (enum: INTERIOR, EXTERIOR, TECHNOLOGY, SAFETY)
         - title (string)
         - description (text)
         - icon (string, optional) — icon identifier
         - sortOrder (int)
      
      5. **TestDriveBooking**
         - id (uuid)
         - firstName, lastName (string)
         - email (string)
         - phone (string)
         - modelId (FK to VehicleModel)
         - dealershipId (FK to Dealership)
         - preferredDate (date)
         - gdprConsent (boolean)
         - status (enum: NEW, CONTACTED, SCHEDULED, COMPLETED, CANCELLED)
         - notes (text, optional) — admin notes
         - createdAt, updatedAt
      
      6. **ContactSubmission**
         - id (uuid)
         - firstName, lastName (string)
         - email (string)
         - subject (string)
         - message (text)
         - gdprConsent (boolean)
         - status (enum: NEW, READ, RESPONDED, ARCHIVED)
         - createdAt
      
      7. **Dealership**
         - id (uuid)
         - name (string)
         - address (string)
         - city (string)
         - state (string, optional)
         - country (string)
         - postalCode (string)
         - phone (string)
         - email (string)
         - latitude (float)
         - longitude (float)
         - isActive (boolean, default true)
         - sortOrder (int)
         - createdAt, updatedAt
      
      Also create seed data:
      - 3 Porsche models: 911 Carrera, Cayenne, Taycan
      - 5-8 specs per model (real Porsche specs from public data)
      - 3-5 features per model
      - 3 dealership locations (use real Porsche dealership cities)
      - Use placeholder image URLs (https://placehold.co/) with appropriate dimensions
      
      Seed script: prisma/seed.ts
    agent: "backend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "critical"
    inputs:
      requirements: "REQ-006"
      context:
        - "Schema must support both API-sourced and manually-seeded data"
        - "Use Prisma enums for status fields and categories"
        - "All IDs should be UUIDs"
        - "Include proper indexes on slug, email, status fields"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-001"
    outputs:
      artifacts:
        - "prisma/schema.prisma"
        - "prisma/seed.ts"
    file_locks:
      - "prisma/schema.prisma"
      - "prisma/seed.ts"
    quality_gates:
      - "type_check"
      - "build"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Foundation & Backend"

  # === TASK 3: Backend API — Vehicle Models ===
  - id: "TASK-003"
    name: "API routes for vehicle models"
    description: |
      Implement Next.js API routes (Route Handlers) for vehicle model data.
      
      Endpoints:
      
      1. **GET /api/models**
         - Returns all active models with basic info (id, name, slug, tagline, startingPrice, category, heroImage)
         - Sorted by sortOrder
         - No pagination needed (max 50 models per scope)
         - Response: { models: VehicleModelSummary[] }
      
      2. **GET /api/models/[slug]**
         - Returns full model detail including images, specs, and features
         - 404 if slug not found or model inactive
         - Response: { model: VehicleModelDetail }
         - Include related images (sorted by sortOrder, grouped by category)
         - Include related specs (sorted by category then sortOrder)
         - Include related features (sorted by category then sortOrder)
      
      3. **GET /api/models/featured**
         - Returns top 3 models (by sortOrder) for homepage featured section
         - Same shape as list endpoint
         - Response: { models: VehicleModelSummary[] }
      
      Implementation details:
      - Use src/app/api/models/route.ts and src/app/api/models/[slug]/route.ts
      - Create shared types in src/types/models.ts
      - Create data access layer in src/lib/dal/models.ts (don't put Prisma calls directly in route handlers)
      - Proper error handling with consistent error response shape: { error: string, code: string }
      - Set appropriate Cache-Control headers (models don't change often — cache for 5 minutes)
      - Input validation on slug parameter (alphanumeric + hyphens only)
    agent: "backend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "high"
    inputs:
      requirements: "REQ-006, REQ-002, REQ-003"
      context:
        - "Data access layer pattern — separate Prisma queries from route handlers"
        - "Consistent error response format across all endpoints"
        - "Cache-Control: public, s-maxage=300, stale-while-revalidate=600"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-002"
    outputs:
      artifacts:
        - "src/app/api/models/route.ts"
        - "src/app/api/models/[slug]/route.ts"
        - "src/app/api/models/featured/route.ts"
        - "src/types/models.ts"
        - "src/lib/dal/models.ts"
    file_locks:
      - "src/app/api/models/"
      - "src/types/models.ts"
      - "src/lib/dal/models.ts"
    quality_gates:
      - "type_check"
      - "lint"
      - "unit_tests"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Foundation & Backend"

  # === TASK 4: Backend API — Form Submissions ===
  - id: "TASK-004"
    name: "API routes for form submissions (test drive + contact)"
    description: |
      Implement API routes for test drive bookings and contact form submissions.
      
      Endpoints:
      
      1. **POST /api/bookings**
         - Accepts test drive booking form data
         - Validates all required fields (firstName, lastName, email, phone, modelId, dealershipId, preferredDate, gdprConsent)
         - Email validation (regex), phone validation (basic format check)
         - gdprConsent must be true (reject if false)
         - preferredDate must be in the future (at least 1 day ahead)
         - Verify modelId and dealershipId exist in database
         - Honeypot field: if 'website' field is filled, silently reject (return 200 but don't save)
         - Rate limiting: max 5 submissions per IP per hour (use in-memory store for simplicity)
         - On success: save to database, send notification email via Resend to admin email (from env var)
         - Response: { success: true, bookingId: string }
      
      2. **POST /api/contact**
         - Accepts contact form data
         - Validates: firstName, lastName, email, subject, message, gdprConsent
         - Same honeypot and rate limiting as bookings
         - On success: save to database, send notification email
         - Response: { success: true }
      
      3. **GET /api/dealerships**
         - Returns all active dealerships
         - Sorted by sortOrder
         - Response: { dealerships: Dealership[] }
         - Public endpoint (needed for form dropdowns)
      
      Implementation:
      - Validation library: zod (add to dependencies)
      - Create validation schemas in src/lib/validations/forms.ts
      - Data access layer in src/lib/dal/submissions.ts and src/lib/dal/dealerships.ts
      - Email templates in src/lib/email/templates.ts (simple HTML templates for admin notifications)
      - Rate limiter utility in src/lib/rate-limit.ts
      - Honeypot utility in src/lib/honeypot.ts
    agent: "backend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "high"
    inputs:
      requirements: "REQ-004, REQ-005, REQ-006"
      context:
        - "Zod for validation — define schemas that can be shared with frontend"
        - "Resend for email — use RESEND_API_KEY env var"
        - "Admin notification email goes to ADMIN_EMAIL env var"
        - "Rate limiting is per-IP, in-memory (acceptable for single-instance deployment)"
        - "Honeypot field named 'website' — if filled, return 200 but don't process"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-002"
    outputs:

        - "src/app/api/contact/route.ts"
        - "src/app/api/dealerships/route.ts"

  # === TASK 5: Admin API Routes ===
  - id: "TASK-005"
    name: "Admin API routes and authentication"
    description: |
      Implement protected admin API routes for managing leads and dealerships.
      
      Auth setup:
      - NextAuth.js with Credentials provider
      - Single admin user: ADMIN_EMAIL and ADMIN_PASSWORD from env vars
      - Session strategy: JWT
      - Protected routes check for valid session
      - Auth config in src/lib/auth.ts (extend from TASK-001 scaffold)
      
      Admin Endpoints (all require authentication):
      
      1. **GET /api/admin/bookings**
         - List all test drive bookings with pagination
         - Query params: page (default 1), limit (default 20), status (optional filter)
         - Include related model name and dealership name
         - Sorted by createdAt descending (newest first)
         - Response: { bookings: BookingWithRelations[], total: number, page: number, totalPages: number }
      
      2. **PATCH /api/admin/bookings/[id]**
         - Update booking status and/or notes
         - Body: { status?: BookingStatus, notes?: string }
         - Response: { booking: BookingWithRelations }
      
      3. **GET /api/admin/contacts**
         - List all contact submissions with pagination
         - Same pagination pattern as bookings
         - Response: { contacts: ContactSubmission[], total: number, page: number, totalPages: number }
      
      4. **PATCH /api/admin/contacts/[id]**
         - Update contact status
         - Body: { status?: ContactStatus }
      
      5. **GET /api/admin/dealerships**
         - List all dealerships (including inactive)
      
      6. **POST /api/admin/dealerships**
         - Create new dealership
         - Validate with zod schema
      
      7. **PATCH /api/admin/dealerships/[id]**
         - Update dealership details
      
      8. **DELETE /api/admin/dealerships/[id]**
         - Soft delete (set isActive = false)
      
      9. **GET /api/admin/stats**
         - Dashboard summary: total bookings (by status), total contacts (by status), total active models
         - Response: { bookings: { total, new, contacted, scheduled }, contacts: { total, new, read }, models: { active } }
      
      Implementation:
      - Auth middleware helper in src/lib/auth-guard.ts — reusable function to check session in route handlers
      - Admin DAL in src/lib/dal/admin.ts
      - Admin types in src/types/admin.ts
      - Pagination utility in src/lib/pagination.ts
    agent: "backend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "high"
    inputs:
      requirements: "REQ-006"
      context:
        - "NextAuth.js Credentials provider — admin user from env vars"
        - "JWT session strategy for stateless auth"
        - "All admin routes must verify session before processing"
        - "Dealership CRUD is needed because human specified dealerships should be editable by their team"
        - "Soft delete pattern for dealerships (never hard delete)"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-002"
        - "TASK-001"
    outputs:
      artifacts:
        - "src/app/api/admin/bookings/route.ts"
        - "src/app/api/admin/bookings/[id]/route.ts"
        - "src/app/api/admin/contacts/route.ts"
        - "src/app/api/admin/contacts/[id]/route.ts"
        - "src/app/api/admin/dealerships/route.ts"
        - "src/app/api/admin/dealerships/[id]/route.ts"
        - "src/app/api/admin/stats/route.ts"
        - "src/lib/auth-guard.ts"
        - "src/lib/dal/admin.ts"
        - "src/types/admin.ts"
        - "src/lib/pagination.ts"
        - "src/app/api/auth/[...nextauth]/route.ts"
    file_locks:
      - "src/app/api/admin/"
      - "src/lib/auth-guard.ts"
      - "src/lib/dal/admin.ts"
      - "src/types/admin.ts"
      - "src/lib/pagination.ts"
      - "src/app/api/auth/"
    quality_gates:
      - "type_check"
      - "lint"
      - "unit_tests"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Foundation & Backend"

  # === TASK 6: Backend Unit Tests (Milestone 1) ===
  - id: "TASK-006"
    name: "Backend unit tests for Milestone 1"
    description: |
      Write comprehensive unit tests for all backend code from Milestone 1.
      
      Test coverage required:
      
      1. **Model API tests** (src/tests/api/models.test.ts)
         - GET /api/models returns all active models
         - GET /api/models/[slug] returns full model detail
         - GET /api/models/[slug] returns 404 for invalid slug
         - GET /api/models/featured returns top 3 models
         - Slug validation rejects invalid characters
      
      2. **Form submission tests** (src/tests/api/submissions.test.ts)
         - POST /api/bookings with valid data succeeds
         - POST /api/bookings with missing fields returns validation errors
         - POST /api/bookings with past date rejected
         - POST /api/bookings with gdprConsent=false rejected
         - POST /api/bookings with honeypot filled returns 200 but doesn't save
         - POST /api/contact with valid data succeeds
         - POST /api/contact validation errors
         - Rate limiting blocks after 5 requests
      
      3. **Admin API tests** (src/tests/api/admin.test.ts)
         - All admin routes return 401 without session
         - GET /api/admin/bookings returns paginated results
         - PATCH /api/admin/bookings/[id] updates status
         - Dealership CRUD operations
         - GET /api/admin/stats returns correct counts
      
      4. **Validation schema tests** (src/tests/lib/validations.test.ts)
         - Booking schema validates correctly
         - Contact schema validates correctly
         - Edge cases: empty strings, invalid emails, XSS attempts in text fields
      
      5. **DAL tests** (src/tests/lib/dal.test.ts)
         - Model queries return expected shapes
         - Submission creation works
         - Pagination utility calculates correctly
      
      Testing setup:
      - Jest with ts-jest
      - Mock Prisma client using jest.mock
      - Mock NextAuth session for admin tests
      - Mock Resend for email tests
      - Test utilities in src/tests/helpers/
    agent: "qa"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "high"
    inputs:
      requirements: "REQ-006"
      context:
        - "Test all backend code from TASK-003, TASK-004, TASK-005"
        - "Mock external dependencies (Prisma, Resend, NextAuth)"
        - "Focus on business logic validation, not framework internals"
        - "Include edge cases and error paths"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-003"
        - "TASK-004"
        - "TASK-005"
    outputs:
      artifacts:
        - "src/tests/api/models.test.ts"
        - "src/tests/api/submissions.test.ts"
        - "src/tests/api/admin.test.ts"
        - "src/tests/lib/validations.test.ts"
        - "src/tests/lib/dal.test.ts"
        - "src/tests/helpers/mocks.ts"
        - "src/tests/helpers/factories.ts"
        - "jest.config.ts"
    file_locks:
      - "src/tests/"
      - "jest.config.ts"
    quality_gates:
      - "type_check"
      - "unit_tests"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Foundation & Backend"

  # MILESTONE 2: Core Pages (Week 2)

  # === TASK 7: Shared UI Components ===
  - id: "TASK-007"
    name: "Shared UI component library"
    description: |
      Build the reusable component library that all pages will use. These are the building blocks.
      
      Design system (inspired by porsche.com):
      - Dark theme: backgrounds in #0a0a0a to #1a1a1a, white/light text
      - Premium feel: generous padding, subtle borders, minimal border-radius
      - Typography: Outfit for headings (bold, uppercase for nav), Inter for body
      - Accent: warm gold (#c4a35a) for CTAs and highlights
      - Transitions: smooth 300ms on hover states
      
      Components to build:
      
      1. **Header/Navigation** (src/components/layout/Header.tsx)
         - Fixed/sticky header with transparent-to-solid on scroll
         - Logo (text-based "PORSCHE" or SVG placeholder)
         - Nav links: Models, About, Contact
         - CTA button: "Book a Test Drive"
         - Mobile hamburger menu with slide-out drawer
         - Active link highlighting
      
      2. **Footer** (src/components/layout/Footer.tsx)
         - 4-column layout: Brand info, Quick Links, Models, Contact
         - Social media icon links (placeholder hrefs)
         - Copyright notice
         - Privacy/Terms links (placeholder)
      
      3. **Button** (src/components/ui/Button.tsx)
         - Variants: primary (gold bg), secondary (outlined), ghost (text only)
         - Sizes: sm, md, lg
         - States: default, hover, active, disabled, loading
         - As link variant (renders as <a> or Next Link)
      
      4. **Card** (src/components/ui/Card.tsx)
         - Vehicle card variant: image, name, tagline, price, CTA
         - Hover effect: subtle scale + shadow
         - Responsive sizing
      
      5. **Section** (src/components/ui/Section.tsx)
         - Wrapper component for page sections
         - Props: dark/light background, padding size, max-width
         - Optional section title + subtitle
      
      6. **Container** (src/components/ui/Container.tsx)
         - Max-width wrapper with responsive padding
      
      7. **Input** (src/components/ui/Input.tsx)
         - Text input with label, error state, helper text
         - Dark theme styling
         - Variants: text, email, tel, textarea
      
      8. **Select** (src/components/ui/Select.tsx)
         - Dropdown select with label and error state
         - Dark theme styling
      
      9. **DatePicker** (src/components/ui/DatePicker.tsx)
         - Simple date input (native HTML date input styled for dark theme)
         - Min date prop (for future-only dates)
      
      10. **Checkbox** (src/components/ui/Checkbox.tsx)
          - Styled checkbox with label (for GDPR consent)
          - Error state
      
      11. **Badge** (src/components/ui/Badge.tsx)
          - Status badges for admin (NEW, CONTACTED, etc.)
          - Color-coded by status
      
      12. **LoadingSpinner** (src/components/ui/LoadingSpinner.tsx)
          - Animated spinner for loading states
      
      13. **ScrollReveal** (src/components/ui/ScrollReveal.tsx)
          - Wrapper that animates children on scroll into view
          - Uses Intersection Observer
          - Variants: fade-up, fade-in, slide-left, slide-right
      
      All components must:
      - Be fully typed with TypeScript (exported prop interfaces)
      - Use forwardRef where appropriate
      - Support className prop for overrides
      - Be accessible (proper ARIA attributes, keyboard navigation)
    agent: "frontend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "critical"
    inputs:
      requirements: "REQ-001, REQ-002, REQ-003, REQ-004, REQ-005, REQ-007"
      context:
        - "These components are used across ALL pages — build them first"
        - "Dark premium theme inspired by porsche.com/international/"
        - "Tailwind CSS for all styling — no CSS modules or styled-components"
        - "Mobile-first responsive design"
        - "All components must be accessible (WCAG 2.1 AA)"
      dependencies:
        - "TASK-001"
    outputs:
      artifacts:
        - "src/components/layout/Header.tsx"
        - "src/components/layout/Footer.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/Button.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/Card.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/Section.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/Container.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/Input.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/Select.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/DatePicker.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/Checkbox.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/Badge.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/LoadingSpinner.tsx"
        - "src/components/ui/ScrollReveal.tsx"
    file_locks:
      - "src/components/layout/"
      - "src/components/ui/"
    quality_gates:
      - "type_check"
      - "lint"
      - "build"
    human_checkpoint: false
    milestone: "Core Pages"

  # === TASK 8: Homepage ===
  - id: "TASK-008"
    name: "Homepage implementation"
    description: |
      Build the homepage — the primary landing page and brand experience entry point.
      
      Sections (top to bottom):
      
      1. **Hero Section**
         - Full-viewport height (100vh)
         - Background: large hero image (placeholder for now) with dark overlay gradient
         - Centered content: Brand name, tagline, two CTA buttons ("Explore Models", "Book a Test Drive")
         - Subtle scroll indicator animation at bottom
         - Consider: parallax effect on scroll (optional, performance permitting)
      
      2. **Featured Models Section**
         - Section title: "Our Models" or "The Lineup"
         - 3 vehicle cards in responsive grid (fetched from /api/models/featured)
         - Each card: hero image, model name, tagline, starting price, "Discover" link
         - Cards animate in on scroll (use ScrollReveal)
      
      3. **Brand Story Teaser**
         - Split layout: image left, text right (stacks on mobile)
         - Brief brand story excerpt
         - "Learn More" link to About page
         - Dark background section
      
      4. **Performance Stats Bar**
         - Horizontal bar with 3-4 key stats (e.g., "75+ Years of Innovation", "3 Iconic Models", "200+ Dealerships Worldwide")
         - Counter animation on scroll into view
         - Accent gold color for numbers
      
      5. **Test Drive CTA Section**
         - Full-width section with background image
         - Heading: "Experience the Drive"
         - Subtext + CTA button to test drive booking page
         - Dark overlay on image
      
      Implementation:
      - Server component for initial render (SEO)
      - Client components only where interactivity needed (scroll animations, counter)
      - Fetch featured models server-side using DAL directly (not API call — same server)
      - Use Next.js Image component for all images
      - Page: src/app/page.tsx (update from scaffold)
      - Section components: src/components/home/
    agent: "frontend"
    status: "pending"
    priority: "high"
    inputs:

        - "Server-side render for SEO, client components only for animations"

    outputs:

    file_locks:

    quality_gates:

      - "build"
    human_checkpoint: false
