/**
 * Tests for session-summary.ts
 *
 * Tests the session summary extraction and context block building.
 * The Anthropic API call is mocked — we test the prompt construction and
 * the parsing of the summary response.
 */

import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Mocks
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const mockCreate = vi.hoisted(() => vi.fn());

vi.mock('@anthropic-ai/sdk', () => ({
  default: vi.fn().mockImplementation(() => ({
    messages: { create: mockCreate },
  })),
}));

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Import after mocks
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

import { extractSessionSummary, buildSessionContextBlock } from '../session-summary';

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Tests
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

describe('extractSessionSummary', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    // Ensure API key is present
    process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY = 'test-key';
  });

  it('extracts a summary from orchestrator response', async () => {
    mockCreate.mockResolvedValueOnce({
      content: [{ type: 'text', text: 'Decided to use React+Next.js. Created 5 backend tasks and 3 frontend tasks. Asked about hosting preferences.' }],
      usage: { input_tokens: 500, output_tokens: 50 },
    });

    const result = await extractSessionSummary(
      'action: decompose_and_assign\ntasks:\n  - name: Setup...',
      'Build Marketing Website',
    );

    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
    expect(result!.summary).toContain('React+Next.js');
    expect(result!.timestamp).toBeTruthy();
    expect(result!.extractionTokens.input).toBe(500);
    expect(result!.extractionTokens.output).toBe(50);
  });

  it('merges with previous summary when provided', async () => {
    mockCreate.mockResolvedValueOnce({
      content: [{ type: 'text', text: 'Previously chose React. Now added 3 API tasks for auth.' }],
      usage: { input_tokens: 600, output_tokens: 40 },
    });

    const result = await extractSessionSummary(
      'action: decompose_and_assign\ntasks:\n  - name: Auth API...',
      'Build Marketing Website',
      'Decided to use React+Next.js. Created 5 backend tasks.',
    );

    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
    expect(result!.summary).toContain('Previously chose React');

    // Verify the previous summary was passed in the prompt
    const callArgs = mockCreate.mock.calls[0][0];
    expect(callArgs.messages[0].content).toContain('Previous Session Summary');
    expect(callArgs.messages[0].content).toContain('React+Next.js');
  });

  it('returns null on API failure (non-blocking)', async () => {
    mockCreate.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('API timeout'));

    const result = await extractSessionSummary(
      'some response',
      'Build Website',
    );

    expect(result).toBeNull();
  });

  it('truncates long orchestrator responses to 4000 chars', async () => {
    mockCreate.mockResolvedValueOnce({
      content: [{ type: 'text', text: 'Summary of truncated response.' }],
      usage: { input_tokens: 300, output_tokens: 20 },
    });

    const longResponse = 'x'.repeat(10000);
    await extractSessionSummary(longResponse, 'Build Website');

    const callArgs = mockCreate.mock.calls[0][0];
    // The prompt should contain at most 4000 chars of the response
    const promptContent = callArgs.messages[0].content;
    expect(promptContent.length).toBeLessThan(10000);
  });

  it('uses Haiku model for cost efficiency', async () => {
    mockCreate.mockResolvedValueOnce({
      content: [{ type: 'text', text: 'Summary.' }],
      usage: { input_tokens: 100, output_tokens: 10 },
    });

    await extractSessionSummary('response', 'Task');

    const callArgs = mockCreate.mock.calls[0][0];
    expect(callArgs.model).toBe('claude-haiku-4-5-20251001');
    expect(callArgs.max_tokens).toBe(1024);
    expect(callArgs.temperature).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('buildSessionContextBlock', () => {
  it('returns empty string when no summary exists', () => {
    expect(buildSessionContextBlock(undefined)).toBe('');
    expect(buildSessionContextBlock('')).toBe('');
  });

  it('builds context block with session summary', () => {
    const block = buildSessionContextBlock('Decided to use React. Created 5 tasks.');

    expect(block).toContain('Session State');
    expect(block).toContain('Decided to use React');
    expect(block).toContain('do NOT re-derive');
  });
});

```

## Changes Made

### Phase 1: Bug Fixes

**Bug 1 — YAML Truncation** (`orchestrator-output-parser.ts`)
- Added `repairTruncatedYaml()` helper that drops the last incomplete YAML entry
- Added truncation-aware fallback in `extractYamlContent()` — when ```` ```yaml ```` exists but no closing ```` ``` ````, it takes content to end of string and repairs it
- 4 new tests covering truncated YAML scenarios

**Bug 2 — UUID LIKE operator** (`orchestrator-command-executor.ts`)
- Replaced `like(tasks.id, ...)` with `sql\`${tasks.id}::text LIKE ...\`` at both call sites (lines 59 and 310)
- Removed unused `like` import, added `sql` import

### Phase 3: Session Summary

**New file** (`session-summary.ts`)
- `extractSessionSummary()` — calls Haiku to extract a compact summary from orchestrator responses (~$0.001 per call)
- `buildSessionContextBlock()` — builds the prompt section for injection
- Merges with previous summary when one exists (accumulative)
- Non-blocking — returns null on failure, orchestrator continues normally

**Integration** (`orchestrator-agent.ts`)
- After each run that produces tasks or questions, extracts session summary
- Persists to `task.context.sessionSummary`
- On re-run, injects into prompt as "Session State" section with instruction to continue from previous decisions

**Schema** (`schema.ts`)
- Added `sessionSummary?: string` to task context type

### Phase 4: MAX_TOKENS

**`orchestrator-agent.ts`**
- Changed default from 16384 to 32768
- Added `ORCHESTRATOR_MAX_TOKENS` env var override (falls back to `CLAUDE_MAX_TOKENS`, then 32768)

### Test Results
- **412 tests pass**, 0 failures
- 11 new tests added (4 truncated YAML + 7 session summary)

    11→  activeTask: { id: string; name: string; status: string } | null;
    12→  recentTask: { id: string; name: string; status: string } | null;
    13→}
    14→
    15→interface ProjectActivityData {
    16→  projectId: string;
    17→  projectName: string;
    18→  projectStatus: string;
    19→  agents: Record<string, AgentActivity>;
    20→  totalTasks: number;
    21→  completedTasks: number;
    22→  runningTasks: number;
    23→  pendingTasks: number;
    24→  failedTasks: number;
    25→}
    26→
    27→interface ProjectActivityPanelProps {
    28→  activity: ProjectActivityData;
    29→}
    30→
    31→// Pipeline stages in execution order
    32→const PIPELINE_STAGES = [
    33→  { key: "scoper", label: "Scoper" },
    34→  { key: "orchestrator", label: "Orchestrator" },
    35→  { key: "backend", label: "Backend" },
    36→  { key: "frontend", label: "Frontend" },
    37→  { key: "qa", label: "QA" },
    38→  { key: "client-reporter", label: "Reporter" },
    39→] as const;
    40→
    41→const STATUS_COLORS: Record<AgentStatus, { dot: string; bg: string; text: string }> = {
    42→  running: { dot: "bg-blue-500 animate-pulse", bg: "bg-blue-50", text: "text-blue-700" },
    43→  completed: { dot: "bg-green-500", bg: "bg-green-50", text: "text-green-700" },
    44→  pending: { dot: "bg-yellow-500", bg: "bg-yellow-50", text: "text-yellow-700" },
    45→  failed: { dot: "bg-red-500", bg: "bg-red-50", text: "text-red-700" },
    46→  idle: { dot: "bg-gray-300", bg: "bg-gray-50", text: "text-gray-500" },
    47→};
    48→
    49→export function ProjectActivityPanel({ activity }: ProjectActivityPanelProps) {
    50→  const [logAgent, setLogAgent] = useState<string | null>(null);
    51→
    52→  // Collect all tasks from all agents that have active or recent tasks
    53→  const activeTasks = PIPELINE_STAGES
    54→    .map((stage) => {
    55→      const agent = activity.agents[stage.key];
    56→      if (!agent) return null;
    57→      const task = agent.activeTask || agent.recentTask;
    58→      if (!task) return null;
    59→      return { ...task, agentType: stage.key, agentLabel: stage.label };
    60→    })
    61→    .filter(Boolean) as Array<{
    62→      id: string;
    63→      name: string;
    64→      status: string;
    65→      agentType: string;
    66→      agentLabel: string;
    67→    }>;
    68→
    69→  return (
    70→    <div className="space-y-4">
    71→      {/* Agent Pipeline */}
    72→      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 p-4">
    73→        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">Agent Pipeline</h3>
    74→        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
    75→          {PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage, i) => {
    76→            const agent = activity.agents[stage.key];
    77→            const status: AgentStatus = agent?.status ?? "idle";
    78→            const colors = STATUS_COLORS[status];
    79→
    80→            return (
    81→              <div key={stage.key} className="flex items-center">
    82→                {i > 0 && (
    83→                  <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-300 flex-shrink-0 mx-0.5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>
    84→                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" />
    85→                  </svg>
    86→                )}
    87→                <div
    88→                  className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md ${colors.bg} min-w-0`}
    89→                >
    90→                  <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full flex-shrink-0 ${colors.dot}`} />
    91→                  <span className={`text-xs font-medium whitespace-nowrap ${colors.text}`}>
    92→                    {stage.label}
    93→                  </span>
    94→                  {agent && agent.taskCount > 0 && (
    95→                    <span className={`text-xs ${colors.text} opacity-60`}>
    96→                      ({agent.taskCount})
    97→                    </span>
    98→                  )}
    99→                </div>
   100→              </div>
   101→            );
   102→          })}
   103→        </div>
   104→      </div>
   105→
   106→      {/* Task Summary + Active Tasks */}
   107→      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
   108→        {/* Active Tasks Table */}
   109→        <div className="lg:col-span-3 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
   110→          <div className="px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-200">
   111→            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900">Tasks by Agent</h3
... (truncated)

```
     1→import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
     2→import { db } from "@/lib/db";
     3→import { tasks } from "@soloenterprise/db/schema";
     4→import { eq, and, desc } from "drizzle-orm";
     5→import { readFileSync, existsSync } from "fs";
     6→import { resolve } from "path";
     7→import { getWorkerStatus, getAllWorkerStatuses } from "@soloenterprise/core/services";
     8→
     9→function readLogSafe(taskId: string): string {
    10→  const logPath = resolve(
    11→    process.cwd(),
    12→    "packages/core/generated/tasks",
    13→    taskId,
    14→    "task.log"
    15→  );
    16→  if (!existsSync(logPath)) return "";
    17→  try {
    18→    return readFileSync(logPath, "utf-8");
    19→  } catch {
    20→    return "";
    21→  }
    22→}
    23→
    24→/**
    25→ * Signal workers to flush buffered logs before reading.
    26→ */
    27→async function signalWorkersFlush(agentType?: string): Promise<void> {
    28→  try {
    29→    const pidsSignaled = new Set<number>();
    30→
    31→    if (agentType) {
    32→      const status = await getWorkerStatus(agentType as any);
    33→      if (status.status === "running" && status.pid) {
    34→        try {
    35→          process.kill(status.pid, "SIGUSR1");
    36→          pidsSignaled.add(status.pid);
    37→        } catch {
    38→          // Worker may have exited
    39→        }
    40→      }
    41→    } else {
    42→      const statuses = await getAllWorkerStatuses();
    43→      for (const status of Object.values(statuses)) {
    44→        if (status.status === "running" && status.pid && !pidsSignaled.has(status.pid)) {
    45→          try {
    46→            process.kill(status.pid, "SIGUSR1");
    47→            pidsSignaled.add(status.pid);
    48→          } catch {
    49→            // Worker may have exited
    50→          }
    51→        }
    52→      }
    53→    }
    54→
    55→    if (pidsSignaled.size > 0) {
    56→      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
    57→    }
    58→  } catch {
    59→    // Ignore signal errors
    60→  }
    61→}
    62→
    63→/**
    64→ * GET /api/projects/:id/logs?agentType=backend
    65→ *
    66→ * Returns combined logs for all tasks in a project, optionally filtered by agent type.
    67→ * Follows the same pattern as GET /api/workers/all/logs.
    68→ */
    69→export async function GET(
    70→  request: Request,
    71→  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
    72→) {
    73→  try {
    74→    const { id } = await params;
    75→    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    76→    const agentType = searchParams.get("agentType");
    77→
    78→    // Signal workers to flush log buffers
    79→    await signalWorkersFlush(agentType || undefined);
    80→
    81→    // Build query conditions
    82→    const conditions = [eq(tasks.projectId, id)];
    83→    if (agentType) {
    84→      conditions.push(eq(tasks.agentType, agentType as any));
    85→    }
    86→
    87→    // Fetch tasks for this project
    88→    const projectTasks = await db.query.tasks.findMany({
    89→      where: and(...conditions),
    90→      orderBy: [desc(tasks.updatedAt)],
    91→      columns: { id: true, name: true, status: true, agentType: true },
    92→    });
    93→
    94→    if (projectTasks.length === 0) {
    95→      return NextResponse.json({
    96→        logs: "",
    97→        taskCount: 0,
    98→        isRunning: false,
    99→      });
   100→    }
   101→
   102→    const sections: string[] = [];
   103→    let anyRunning = false;
   104→
   105→    for (const task of projectTasks) {
   106→      const logs = readLogSafe(task.id);
   107→      if (!logs) continue;
   108→
   109→      const isRunning = ["running", "queued"].includes(task.status);
   110→      if (isRunning) anyRunning = true;
   111→
   112→      sections.push(
   113→        `${"=".repeat(80)}\n` +
   114→        `[${task.agentType.toUpperCase()}] ${task.name} (${task.status})\n` +
   115→        `Task: ${task.id}\n` +
   116→        `${"=".repeat(80)}\n` +
   117→        logs
   118→      );
   119→    }
   120→
   121→    return NextResponse.json({
   122→      logs: sections.join("\n\n"),
   123→      taskCount: projectTasks.length,
   124→      isRunning: anyRunning,
   125→      taskName: agentType
   126→        ? `${agentType} logs for project`
   127→        : "All agent logs for project",
   128→      taskStatus: anyRunning ? "running" : null,
   129→    });
   130→  } catch (error) {
   131→    console.error("Failed to read project logs:", error);
   132→    return NextResponse.json(
   133→      { error: "Failed to read logs" },
   134→      { status: 500 }
   135→    );
   136→  }
   137→}
   138→
